- **leadbay_new_leads**: Guided net-new lead delivery — turn a described need ("gyms around Dallas that would buy our flooring") into ICP-perfect NEW companies with qualification evidence and the right contact, via leadbay_find_new_leads. Trigger when the user DESCRIBES who they want: "get me N companies that &lt;profile&gt;", "we're entering &lt;market&gt;". A bare "find me new leads" with no profile, and "today's leads", are the daily lens batch — leadbay_pull_leads. "Qualify these companies I have" is leadbay_qualify_leads. - **leadbay_outreach_session**: Run an outreach session over ANY set of leads: follow-ups, a Discover lens, or a campaign. - **leadbay_plan_tour_in_city**: Use whenever the user names a city they'll be in and asks who to see — "I'm in SF next Tuesday, who's worth meeting?", "I'm going to Berlin — who should I visit?", "plan my &lt;city&gt; tour".
